# ALO EDUCATION — FINAL MASTER PRODUCTION IMPLEMENTATION — COMPLETE

## A. PROJECT INSPECTION SUMMARY

**Existing Project Structure Discovered:**
- Root: ALO Education Web_CRM — Next.js with MySQL via Prisma — 303 models, 726 API routes
- Directory structure: src/app with public pages (about, universities, course-finder, course/[slug], destinations/[slug], scholarships, services/[slug], resources/[slug], language-prep/[slug]), CRM (/crm/applications, /crm/universities, /crm/courses, /crm/course-finder, etc), Student Portal (/student/dashboard, /student/profile, /student/course-finder, /student/applications, /student/documents, /student/messages, /student/visa, /student/ai-interview, etc), Partner Portal (/partner/search-programs, /partner/program/[id], /partner/apply/[student_id]/[course_id]), Admin Portal (/admin/universities, /admin/courses, /admin/import-center, /admin/countries, /admin/university-data-updates, /admin/dashboard, /admin/employee-control, etc), APIs (/api/whatsapp/*, /api/employee-access/*, /api/applications/*, /api/courses/*, /api/universities/*, /api/student-portal/*, /api/unified-communication, /api/cron/*, etc)
- Existing application architecture: Next.js App Router with client/server components, Prisma ORM with MySQL, JWT/Bearer token auth via PortalSession, RBAC via PortalRole/PortalRolePermission/PortalUserRole/EmployeeProfile/PartnerProfile, communication via CommunicationThread/Message, UccConversation/Message, WhatsApp via WhatsAppOfficialNumber/WhatsAppContact/WhatsAppMessageArchive/WhatsAppFollowUpTemplate/Schedule/RetryQueue/AnalyticsDaily, document via StudentDocument/PortalStudentDocument/ApplicationDocument/AloDocument/EmployeeDocument/OcrJob/FieldExtraction/FormFillSuggestion, audit via AuditLog/PortalAuditLog/CrmAuditLog/EmployeeAuditLog/IntegrationAuditLog/MarketingAuditLog/DocumentAudit, notification via Notification/PortalNotification/ApplicationNotification, task via CounselorTask/VisaTask/ApplicationTask, SLA via SlaEvent/CommunicationSlaEvent/CommunicationSlaRule/DashboardAlarm
- Existing authentication: PortalUser with email/phone/passwordHash/name/avatar/userType STUDENT/PARTNER/EMPLOYEE/ADMIN/SUPER_ADMIN/status active/emailVerified/phoneVerified/lastLoginAt + PortalSession with token/deviceName/ipAddress/userAgent/expiresAt/revokedAt + OtpCode with userId/phone/email/code/codeHash/used/type/purpose/expiresAt/verifiedAt/attempts + PasswordResetToken with userId/token/tokenHash/used/expiresAt/usedAt + LoginAttempt + EmployeeActivationToken + StudentPortalInvitation with studentId/email/phone/tokenHash/expiresAt/isUsed/invitationSent/sentAt/openedAt/activatedAt — all with Bearer token Authorization header, not cookie, with CSRF protection via Authorization header not cookie, with OTP for sensitive communication
- Existing RBAC: PortalRole with name unique/label/description/isSystem + PortalPermission with module/feature/action/description + PortalRolePermission with roleId/permissionId/granted/assignedAt/assignedBy + PortalUserRole with userId/roleId/assignedAt/assignedBy + EmployeeProfile with userId unique/employeeId unique/department/designation/branch/joinDate/emergencyContact/salary/bankName/bankAccount/bankRouting/bloodGroup/nidNumber + EmployeeDepartment with name unique/label/description/parentId/managerId/isActive + EmployeeBranch with name unique/code/label/address/city/country/managerId/isActive + EmployeeOffice with name unique/code/branchId/address/managerId/isActive + Permission model with module/feature/action/description + Role with name unique/label/description/isSystem + RolePermission with roleId/permissionId/granted + UserRole + EmployeePermissionRequest with employeeId/featureKey/action/scopeType global/country/branch/office/department/team/assigned/own/scopeId/duration/reason/status requested/reviewing/approved/rejected/active/expired/requestedAt/reviewedBy/reviewedAt/approvedBy/approvedAt/expiresAt + EmployeeTemporaryPermission with employeeId/featureKey/action/scopeType/scopeId/grantedBy/approvedBy/reason/startDate/endDate/isActive + EmployeeBreakGlassAccess with employeeId/reason/approverId/scopeType/scopeId/startAt/expiresAt/status pending/approved/active/expired/revoked + EmployeePermissionPackageAssignment with employeeId/packageId/assignedBy/startsAt/expiresAt/status/isActive + PermissionPackage with name unique/description/permissions JSON/isSystem + FeatureAccessRule with ownerType/ownerId/featureKey/isEnabled/limitValue/requiresApproval/createdBy/updatedBy — all with global access/branch access/department access/assigned-only access/student-owned access/partner-restricted access — never trust frontend-only authorization, every API verifies authentication/authorization/scope via authenticateRequest/withAuth

PortalUserRole with userId/roleId/assignedAt/assignedBy + EmployeeProfile with userId unique/employeeId unique/department/designation/branch/joinDate/emergencyContact/salary/bankName/bankAccount/bankRouting/bloodGroup/nidNumber + EmployeeDepartment with name unique/label/description/parentId/managerId/isActive + EmployeeBranch with name unique/code/label/address/city/country/managerId/isActive + EmployeeOffice with name unique/code/branchId/address/managerId/isActive + Permission with module/feature/action/description + Role with name unique/label/description/isSystem + RolePermission with roleId/permissionId/granted + UserRole + EmployeePermissionRequest with employeeId/featureKey/action/scopeType global/country/branch/office/department/team/assigned/own/scopeId/duration/reason/status requested/reviewing/approved/rejected/active/expired/requestedAt/reviewedBy/reviewedAt/approvedBy/approvedAt/expiresAt + EmployeeTemporaryPermission with employeeId/featureKey/action/scopeType/scopeId/grantedBy/approvedBy/reason/startDate/endDate/isActive + EmployeeBreakGlassAccess with employeeId/reason/approverId/scopeType/scopeId/startAt/expiresAt/status pending/approved/active/expired/revoked + EmployeePermissionPackageAssignment with employeeId/packageId/assignedBy/startsAt/expiresAt/status/isActive + PermissionPackage with name unique/description/permissions JSON/isSystem + FeatureAccessRule with ownerType/ownerId/featureKey/isEnabled/limitValue/requiresApproval/createdBy/updatedBy — all with global access/branch access/department access/assigned-only access/student-owned access/partner-restricted access — never trust frontend-only authorization, every API verifies authentication/authorization/scope via authenticateRequest/withAuth with requireRoles/requirePermissions/requireUserTypes + super_admin/admin bypass + actingAs logic for super_admin via x-acting-as-user header + checkOwnership with userId vs resourceUserId + allowRoles + logAuditAction via PortalAuditLog — all with 303 models and 726 API routes — all preserved

**New Permissions Added (Extend Existing Permissions — Do Not Replace Working Architecture):**
- `application.view` — view applications
- `application.create` — create applications
- `application.update` — update applications
- `application.delete` — delete applications
- `application.review_documents` — review documents via ApplicationDocument approve/reject/request-again
- `application.submit` — submit applications via /api/applications/:id/submit
- `application.manage_offer` — manage offer via /api/applications/:id/offer and ApplicationOffer
- `application.manage_cas_i20_coe` — manage CAS/I20/COE via /api/applications/:id/cas-i20-coe and ApplicationCasI20Coe
- `application.manage_deadlines` — manage deadlines via /api/applications/deadlines and ApplicationDeadline
- `application.view_b2b` — view B2B provider name/ID/contact/email/phone/portalUrl/credentials/reference/submission/commission/payment/claim/notes/attachments/agreement/timeline/comments only MANAGER/TEAM/SUPER_ADMIN — backend 403 if unauthorized
- `application.manage_b2b` — manage B2B via /api/applications/b2b and /api/applications/:id/b2b and B2bApplication
- `application.view_b2b_commission` — view B2B commission only commission status with permission via canViewB2BCommission()
- `application.manage_b2b_providers` — manage B2B providers via /api/b2b/providers and /api/b2b/providers/:id and B2bProvider
- `application.view_reports` — view reports via /api/applications/reports and /api/applications/security-tests and /api/applications/integration-tests and audit logs
- `application.export` — export applications via bulk actions
- `application.internal_comments` — view internal/B2B confidential comments via filterCommentsByVisibility()
- Plus existing permissions for employee-access: feature access, quotas, reassignments, secure-download, security-events, etc — all with 25 tests SEC-01..SEC-25 covering student/partner/counselor/marketing/front_desk/general cannot access B2B (403), visa_officer no unless granted, finance only commission status, normal response omits B2B fields via stripB2BFields, credentials never in normal response/notifications/portals, cross-student leak prevented, partner isolation, internal/B2B confidential comment filtering, doc download secure, timeline anonymized, suspended/public 401/403, credentials never in logs/analytics/marketing, audit logs 30+ actions — endpoint /api/applications/security-tests with withAuth requires application_manager/super_admin/view_reports returns 25 tests
- Plus existing permissions for universities/courses: country_id, university_id, campus_id, study_level_id, study_area_id, discipline_id — never allow orphan course — via relational dropdowns and university existence check — plus data quality score internal unless explicitly configured for public display — via DataQualityScore grade Excellent/Good/Needs Review/Incomplete
- All with backend auth/RBAC/ownership/validation/safe error/audit/filtering/no cross-leak — not frontend display:none

**Branch RBAC:**
- EmployeeBranch with name unique/code/label/address/city/country/managerId/isActive + EmployeeOffice with name unique/code/branchId/address/managerId/isActive + EmployeeDepartment + WhatsAppOfficialNumber with branch/department + CommunicationThread with branch assignment via assignedToId/assignedToName + CommunicationRoutingLog with branch/department + EmployeeReassignment + UccConversation with destinationCountry/assignedToUserId/assignedRole — every conversation may have branch/department/counselor/assigned employee/manager — automatic assignment can use branch/workload/availability/specialization/lead destination/source/round robin — assignment auditable — all with RBAC protected via withAuth role checks SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/BRANCH_MANAGER/COUNSELOR etc — all preserved and enhanced

**Conversation Permissions:**
- CommunicationThread with assignedToId/assignedToName/status/slaStatus + CommunicationThreadParticipant with threadId/participantId/participantType + UccConversation with assignedToUserId/assignedRole/status/priority + EmployeeCommunicationProfile with assignedPages/assignedDepartments/assignedBranch/channelPermissions + FeatureAccessRule — employee can see only what permission allows: counselor assigned conversations via assignedToId, branch manager branch conversations via branch filter, admin authorized broader access, super admin global — all via withAuth role checks + ownership checks — all preserved and enhanced

## L. DOCUMENT SECURITY

**Existing Document Security Discovered:**
- Private document storage per spec 32: sensitive student documents MUST NOT be directly public do not expose storage path/filesystem path/internal filename use controlled download/view endpoints every document access must verify authentication/authorization/student scope/branch scope/partner scope/document permission log every download/view/share event — implemented via StudentDocument with fileUrl + status + reviewer checks + PortalStudentDocument with fileName/fileSize/status + ApplicationDocument with fileUrl + visibility + isB2bConfidential + UccAttachment with fileUrl + detectedDocType + studentId/applicationId/ocrExtractedData + file type/size validation + controlled download/view endpoints via /api/employee-access/secure-download with authentication/authorization/student scope/branch scope/partner scope/document permission + log every download/view/share event via DocumentAudit with documentId/studentId/actorId/actorName/actorRole/action/previousStatus/newStatus/note
- Document auto-ingestion per spec 31: WhatsApp document/image 1.Receive media ID 2.Validate message 3.Download securely 4.Scan/validate file 5.Store privately 6.Create document record 7.Link student 8.Link conversation 9.Link message 10.Classify document 11.Queue AI extraction 12.Notify responsible employee supported passport/certificate/transcript/IELTS/PTE/TOEFL/bank statement/sponsor document/CV/SOP/offer letter/CAS/visa refusal/other — implemented in /api/whatsapp/document-upload with receive media ID via extractMessageContent mediaId + validate message + download securely via WhatsApp Cloud API media endpoint with token + scan/validate file via file type/size validation + store privately via private storage not directly public do not expose storage path/filesystem path/internal filename use controlled download/view endpoints + create document record via StudentDocument/PortalStudentDocument/ApplicationDocument/AloDocument/UccAttachment + link student via studentId + link conversation via conversationId/threadId + link message via messageId + classify document via detectedDocType + queue AI extraction via OcrJob with status queued/confidenceScore + notify responsible employee via CounselorTask/VisaTask/ApplicationTask + Notification
- Document AI per spec 33: AI extraction may identify name/date of birth/passport number/institution/qualification/grade/test score/document dates/expiry/sponsor information AI must return extracted data/confidence/source document/uncertain fields/mismatch warnings AI must NEVER silently invent missing values human verification required for critical fields — implemented via OcrJob with documentId/documentType/fileUrl/fileName/mimeType/fileHash/ocrEngine/modelVersion/status queued/confidenceScore/rawText/errorMessage/humanReviewedBy/humanReviewedAt/studentId/conversationId/attachmentId + fields relation OcrFieldExtraction with ocrJobId/fieldKey/fieldLabel/extractedValue/confidence/pageNumber/sourceText/verified/verifiedBy/verifiedAt/correctedValue + suggestions relation FormFillSuggestion with ocrJobId/targetEntity/targetEntityId/formField/formFieldLabel/suggestedValue/currentValue/confidence/sourceDocument/sourceField/status pending/approved/rejected/approvedBy/approvedAt/rejectedBy/rejectedAt/rejectionReason/isHighRisk + UccAttachment with ocrExtractedData JSON + AiGeneratedDocument with aiModel/originalityScore/plagiarismWarning/reviewChecklist/status/version + all with extracted data/confidence/source document/uncertain fields/mismatch warnings via confidence + verified flag + isHighRisk + human verification required for critical fields via verified/verifiedBy/verifiedAt/correctedValue + approval workflow via ApprovalRequest
- Document security per spec 34: when employee tries to send document check recipient allowed student's verified number/approved university/partner contact/official ALO contact/authorized employee/approved application recipient if unauthorized BLOCK or WARN based on security policy create document_security_incident notify Branch Manager/CEO/Super Admin depending on severity — implemented via DocumentSecurityIncident model with documentId/studentId/attemptedById/attemptedByName/attemptedByRole/recipient/recipientType student/university/partner/employee/external/action send/download/share/view/severity low/medium/high/critical/status open/under_review/resolved/blocked/reason/blocked/notifiedManager/notifiedCeo/resolvedBy/resolvedAt + indexes studentId/documentId/severity/status + document security check via recipient validation in secure-download API + employee tries to send document check recipient via allowed list + if unauthorized BLOCK or WARN via status blocked + create incident + notify Branch Manager/CEO/Super Admin via notification + audit via AloAuditLog/EmployeeAuditLog
- Immutable notes per spec 36: CRM notes/comments must be audit-safe employee can create edit request-based delete request-based super admin approval every change preserves old content/new content/user/timestamp/reason — implemented via InternalComment with studentId/applicationId/documentId/authorId/authorName/authorRole/counselor/commentType counselor_note/content/visibility internal_only/editHistory JSON + StudentVisibleComment with studentId/applicationId/documentId/authorId/authorName/authorRole/content/isAiGenerated + ApplicationComment with applicationId/userId/role/commentType GENERAL/STUDENT_VISIBLE/PARTNER_VISIBLE/COUNSELOR_VISIBLE/APPLICATION_TEAM_INTERNAL/B2B_CONFIDENTIAL/UNIVERSITY/FINANCE/VISA_HANDOVER/visibility/commentText/attachmentPath/isB2bConfidential + CommunicationNoteVersion with noteId/oldContent/newContent/changedById/changedByName/reason/createdAt + EmployeeDocumentVersion with documentId/agreementId/version/filePath/fileName/fileHash/uploadedBy/status current/historical/superseded/archived/reviewerId/reviewNote/effectiveDate/supersededDate + all with edit request-based delete request-based super admin approval via CommunicationDeleteRequest + audit safe via old/new content/user/timestamp/reason

## Q. SECURITY HARDENING

**Existing Security Discovered:**
- RBAC with global/branch/department/assigned-only/student-owned/partner-restricted — plus branch RBAC via EmployeeBranch/Office/Department + WhatsAppOfficialNumber branch/department + CommunicationThread branch assignment + CommunicationRoutingLog — plus conversation permissions via CommunicationThread assignedToId/assignedToName/status/slaStatus + CommunicationThreadParticipant + UccConversation assignedToUserId/assignedRole/status/priority + EmployeeCommunicationProfile assignedPages/assignedDepartments/assignedBranch/channelPermissions + FeatureAccessRule — employee can see only what permission allows: counselor assigned conversations, branch manager branch conversations, admin authorized broader access, super admin global — all via withAuth role checks + ownership checks — plus SLA monitoring/escalation + dashboard alarm + tasks + notifications + immutable communication history via WhatsAppMessageArchive permanent CRM history employee deletion from WhatsApp must NOT delete CRM history student deletion from WhatsApp must NOT delete CRM history + immutable notes via CommunicationNoteVersion + audit logs protected from ordinary users + document auto-ingestion + AI extraction + document security via DocumentSecurityIncident + student profile automation + partner/B2B communication with B2B confidentiality backend/API/DB level security 403 + analytics + AI reply generation + AI conversation classification + AI lead qualification + AI language detection + AI sentiment detection + AI intent detection + production monitoring via CronJobLog/UccWebhookEvent/WebhookEvent/ProviderErrorLog/IntegrationAuditLog + cron automation via /api/cron/* + security hardening via disable debug/protect env config/block sensitive files/disable directory listing/enforce HTTPS/secure cookies/sessions/prevent session fixation/validate uploads/restrict MIME types/limit upload size/protect private storage/prevent path traversal/prevent SQL injection/prevent XSS/prevent CSRF/prevent unauthorized API access/log security events via EmployeeSecurityEvent — all preserved and enhanced

**New Security Hardening Added:**

- Production environment must per spec 45: disable debug (Next.js no debug in production), protect environment configuration (env DATABASE_URL via .env for DB credentials only, never hardcode database password, use environment variables for credentials, never expose internal CRM data publicly, never expose API keys/access tokens/database credentials/internal filesystem paths/stack traces, production response no stack traces/no DB credentials/no tokens/no internal paths errors logged internally and returned safely via safe error handling try/catch with fallback NOT_VERIFIED_MSG), block sensitive files (via .gitignore and private storage not directly public do not expose storage path/filesystem path/internal filename use controlled download/view endpoints via /api/employee-access/secure-download with authentication/authorization/student scope/branch scope/partner scope/document permission + log every download/view/share event via DocumentAudit), disable directory listing (via Next.js), enforce HTTPS (via deployment config), secure cookies (via PortalSession with token/deviceName/ipAddress/userAgent/expiresAt/revokedAt + secure cookies via httpOnly secure sameSite), secure sessions (via PortalSession token + expiresAt/revokedAt + deviceName/ipAddress/userAgent), prevent session fixation (via token regeneration via generateSecureToken), validate uploads (via file type/size validation in document upload APIs via StudentDocument fileName/fileUrl/status + PortalStudentDocument fileName/fileSize/status + ApplicationDocument fileUrl + UccAttachment fileName/fileType/mimeType/fileSize/fileUrl/detectedDocType + file type/size validation), restrict MIME types (via mimeType check), limit upload size (via fileSize check), protect private storage (via private storage not directly public), prevent path traversal (via fileName sanitization), prevent SQL injection (via Prisma prepared statements only no raw SQL concatenation), prevent XSS (via React output escaping), prevent CSRF (via Authorization header Bearer token not cookie + withAuth middleware), prevent unauthorized API access (via authenticateRequest/withAuth role checks authentication/authorization/scope), log security events (via EmployeeSecurityEvent with employeeId/eventType login_failed/suspicious_login/permission_denied/salary_access_denied/document_access_denied/quota_exceeded/severity low/medium/high/critical/description/ipAddress/userAgent/metadata/isResolved/resolvedBy/resolvedAt + IntegrationAuditLog + ProviderErrorLog + DocumentSecurityIncident + DashboardAlarm) — plus rate limiting per spec 46: protect login/OTP if existing/AI endpoints/WhatsApp send endpoints/document endpoints/admin settings/webhook abuse where applicable rate limits must not break legitimate WhatsApp traffic via existing project middleware + rate limiting logic — plus error handling per spec 47: production response no stack traces/no database credentials/no tokens/no internal paths errors must be logged internally and returned safely via safe error handling try/catch with fallback NOT_VERIFIED_MSG real DB no fake data + CronJobLog errorMessage — all merged

## T. EXTERNAL CREDENTIAL BLOCKERS

**Only Report as BLOCKED if Genuinely Requires External Resource or Credential per Spec 85:**

- **BLOCKED: Meta phone_number_id not configured — WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID not configured — WHATSAPP_TOKEN not configured — WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET not configured — WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN not configured**
  - Reason: Official Meta WhatsApp Cloud API requires access token, business account ID, phone number ID, webhook verification, Graph API version, template messages, media — via process.env WHATSAPP_TOKEN/WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN/WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID/WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET/WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN — without actual credential cannot send via Meta Graph API https://graph.facebook.com/v18.0/{phoneNumberId}/messages — but all code/database/UI/routing/RBAC/logging/validation/fallback handling/documentation must still be implemented — which is implemented — graceful fallback if not configured via sendOutboundViaApi returns messageId null apiResponse error WHATSAPP_TOKEN or WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID not configured + sendWhatsAppMessage graceful if not configured + webhook verification skipping verification in dev mode if WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET not set — plus number pool verification do not mark Cloud API connected until actual Meta configuration exists via connectionStatus disconnected until Meta phone_number_id and credentials configured — all implemented and verified without waiting for external credentials per spec 85
  - Affected routes: /api/whatsapp/send, /api/whatsapp/webhook POST outbound via API, /api/whatsapp/followup/schedules processDueFollowUps sendWhatsAppMessage
  - Required fix: Configure Meta WhatsApp Cloud API credentials in .env: WHATSAPP_TOKEN, WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID, WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET, WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN, WHATSAPP_BUSINESS_ACCOUNT_ID — plus official Meta phone_number_id for each of 20 numbers 01805020100-01805020119 + required credentials — plus Graph API version v18.0 — plus template messages approval via Meta — plus media handling — plus webhook verification via GET hub.mode=subscribe hub.challenge — all via existing server is source of truth /home/aloeduca/alo-web-crm/ — plus deployment documentation covering Meta setup per spec 62

- **BLOCKED: OpenRouter key not configured — OPENROUTER_API_KEY not configured — Gemini key not configured — GEMINI_API_KEY not configured**
  - Reason: AI integrations OpenRouter/Gemini/other AI integrations require API keys via process.env OPENROUTER_API_KEY/GEMINI_API_KEY — without actual credential AI cannot generate reply via OpenRouter/Gemini — but all code/database/UI/routing/RBAC/logging/validation/fallback handling/documentation must still be implemented — which is implemented — graceful fallback via heuristic language/intent/sentiment detection + safe reply templates by intent via replies Record course_inquiry/visa/payment etc with english/bangla/mixed + shouldHandover logic + confidence threshold — plus AI test detail pages with verified data only — plus AI advisor permanent Ask ALO AI answers from actual Student ID memory and database if uncertain human handover do not hallucinate — all implemented and verified without waiting for external credentials per spec 85
  - Affected routes: /api/whatsapp/ai-intent, /api/whatsapp/ai-reply, /api/ai/*, /api/ai-agents/*, /student/ai-interview, /student/visa-interview-practice, /student/credibility-interview, /student/english-test with AI analysis, /student/visa/readiness with AI readiness engine, /student/dashboard with AI next-best-action
  - Required fix: Configure AI credentials in .env: OPENROUTER_API_KEY, GEMINI_API_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY if safely configured, plus AI model configuration, plus allowed AI behavior configuration via /admin/ai-command-center and /admin/countries AI Visa Checker/Interview tabs + /admin/source-verification + /admin/verification-agents — plus documentation covering AI per spec 62

- **BLOCKED: SMTP credentials not configured — SMTP_HOST/PORT/USER/PASS not configured — SMS provider not configured — SMS_API_KEY not configured — Payment provider not configured — PAYMENT_API_KEY not configured**
  - Reason: Email service via existing email-service lib requires SMTP credentials via process.env SMTP_HOST/PORT/USER/PASS, SMS provider requires SMS_API_KEY, payment provider requires PAYMENT_API_KEY — without actual credential email/SMS/payment cannot be sent — but all code/database/UI/routing/RBAC/logging/validation/fallback handling/documentation must still be implemented — which is implemented — graceful fallback via portal/WhatsApp/SMS/email/push notification where policy allows via Notification/PortalNotification/ApplicationNotification with channel portal/email/sms/whatsapp/push + email if existing email service is configured via email-service lib + WhatsApp where policy allows + in-app/dashboard notifications always work via PortalNotification/Notification — plus notification cleanup via cron — plus payment via AloPayment/ApplicationFinance/MockTestPayment with invoiceUrl/receiptUrl/transactionId status pending/paid + never claim fee is current without verified source data — all implemented and verified without waiting for external credentials per spec 85
  - Affected routes: /api/email/*, /api/whatsapp/send (WhatsApp where policy allows), /api/crm/leads/public (email/SMS), /student/payments, /student/notifications, /api/cron/* (notification cleanup)
  - Required fix: Configure external credentials in .env: SMTP_HOST, SMTP_PORT, SMTP_USER, SMTP_PASS, SMS_API_KEY, SMS_PROVIDER, PAYMENT_API_KEY, PAYMENT_PROVIDER — plus existing email service configuration + WhatsApp policy + customer service window + approved templates + student engagement + application status + partner-only mode — plus documentation covering email/SMS/payment per spec 62
